The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. NOT document contents.\n309\t\n310\tIf any agent failed, note the failure and continue with successful documents.\n311\t\n312\tContinue to verify_output.\n313\t</step>\n314\t\n315\t<step name=\"sequential_mapping\" condition=\"Agent tool is NOT available (e.g. Antigravity, Codex)\">\n316\tWhen the `Agent` tool is unavailable, perform codebase mapping sequentially in the current context. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Consider adding sensitive files to Claude Code \"Deny\" permissions\n\nPausing before commit. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - It serves recruiters/hiring managers and the design-engineering community equally: personality on the surface, key information (experience, projects, contact) reachable in seconds.\n8\t\n9\t**Core Value:** The site must feel crafted and memorable (tactile, physics-based motion and strong visual identity) while key info stays instantly accessible, on mobile as much as desktop.\n10\t\n11\t### Constraints\n12\t\n13\t- **Tech stack**: Vite + React + TypeScript + Tailwind — required by motion-primitives and react-spring\n14\t- **Hosting**: GitHub Pages — static output only, needs Actions workflow or a built-output branch\n15\t- **Platform**: Mobile must be excellent — touch interactions, performance on phones\n16\t- **Accessibility/perf**: Assumed baseline: `prefers-reduced-motion` respected, good Lighthouse scores (to confirm with user)\n17\t- **Process**: Detailed documentation before coding; research and surface findings for tooling decisions\n18\t\n19\t<!-- GSD:project-end -->\n20\t\n21\t<!-- GSD:stack-start source:research/STACK.md -->\n22\t\n23\t## Technology Stack\n24\t\n25\t## Bottom Line\n26\t\n27\t## Recommended Stack\n28\t\n29\t### Core Framework\n30\t\n31\t| Technology | Version (verified 2026-10-08) | Purpose | Why | Confidence |\n32\t|------------|-------------------------------|---------|-----|------------|\n33\t| Vite | `^8.3.4` | Build/dev server | Current `latest`. `create-vite` 9.2.1 scaffolds it. No reason to be adventurous in v1. | HIGH |\n36\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | `^6.1.2` | React transform | Required pairing for Vite 8 (peer `vite ^8`). | HIGH |\n37\t| Tailwind CSS | `^4.3.3` | Styling | CSS-first config (`@import \"tailwindcss\"` + `@theme`). No `tailwind.config.js`. motion-primitives' own repo is on Tailwind v4. | HIGH |\n38\t| @tailwindcss/vite | `^4.3.3` | Tailwind plugin | First-party Vite plugin, peer `vite ^5.2 \\|\\| ^6 \\|\\| ^7 \\|\\| ^8`. Family name is `'Inter Variable'`. Realtime Colors takes any Google Font name, and Fontsource packages every Google font the same way, so whatever you pick there installs as `@fontsource-variable/<name>` (or `@fontsource/<name>` for non-variable). | HIGH |\n59\t| realtimecolors.com | web tool | Palette + type exploration | Design tool, not a dependency. See \"How to use the four tools\". | HIGH |\n60\t\n61\t### Supporting Libraries (recommended now)\n62\t\n63\t| Library | Version | Purpose | When to Use |\n64\t|---------|---------|---------|-------------|\n65\t| vite-imagetools | `^12.0.1` | Build-time resize + AVIF/WebP + srcset | Always, for the `images/` and `about-dj.jpg` assets. **Verified**: builds under Vite 8 and emits AVIF/WebP via `?w=400;800&format=avif;webp&as=picture`. Lucide dropped brand icons. Prefer 3 hand-inlined SVGs if only 3-4 are needed (zero deps). |\n68\t\n69\t### Development Tools\n70\t\n71\t| Tool | Purpose | Notes |\n72\t|------|---------|-------|\n73\t| oxlint (scaffold default) | Lint | `create-vite` 9.2.1 ships `oxlint` in `npm run lint`. Keep it. |\n74\t| @types/node | `vite.config.ts` path alias | Needed for `node:path` / `import.meta.dirname`. |\n75\t| Prettier + `prettier-plugin-tailwindcss` | Class sorting | Optional, recommended once Tailwind classes grow. |\n76\t\n77\t## Verified Bundle Costs (measured, gzipped JS, Vite 8 production build)\n78\t\n79\t| Build | JS gz | Delta vs. baseline |\n80\t|-------|-------|--------------------|\n81\t| Baseline: React 19.3 + Tailwind CSS (2.2 kB gz CSS) | 68.6 kB | n/a |\n82\t| + react-spring only (`useSpring`, `useTrail`, `useSprings`) | 85.8 kB | **+17.2 kB** |\n83\t| + motion with TextEffect, Tilt, Magnetic, `MotionConfig` | 121.5 kB | **+52.9 kB** |\n84\t| + both | 138.6 kB | **+70.0 kB** |\n85\t| Lenis (ReactLenis) alone | 74.2 kB | +5.6 kB |\n86\t| OGL (minimal Renderer/Program/Mesh) | 81.4 kB | +12.8 kB |\n87\t| GSAP core + ScrollTrigger | 112.7 kB | +44.1 kB |\n88\t| three + @react-three/fiber (one box) | 309.5 kB | **+240.9 kB** |\n89\t\n90\t- Coexistence costs about +17 kB beyond motion. That is acceptable for a portfolio, but it is the marginal price of the \"both libraries\" decision, so use react-spring where it earns it.\n91\t- Full `motion` component is the heavy part (about 53 kB with presence/spring/gesture features). motion.dev's own figures: `motion` about 34 kB, `m` + `LazyMotion` about 4.6 kB initial (+15 kB `domAnimation`, +25 kB `domMax`), `useAnimate` mini about 2.3 kB. **Optional optimization (not tested):** because motion-primitives is copy-pasted source, you could swap `motion.x` for `m.x` and wrap the app in `LazyMotion`. `AnimatePresence`/`layout`/`drag` need `domMax`, which erodes the saving. Do this only if Lighthouse demands it.\n92\t- Code-split below-the-fold sections with `React.lazy` so the hero ships alone. Any 3D must be lazy-loaded (see Gaps).\n93\t- Not measured: runtime frame rate on a real phone. That belongs in an early performance spike.\n94\t\n95\t## The Four Named Tools\n96\t\n97\t### 1. motion-primitives (ibelick) : USE, as a copy-in source library. Confidence: MEDIUM-HIGH\n98\t\n99\t- Only deps are `motion`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `lucide-react`, plus a `@/lib/utils` `cn()` helper. **No shadcn init required.**\n100\t- Components are served as shadcn-registry JSON at `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` (HTTP 200 verified).\n101\t- The official CLI is `npx motion-primitives@latest add <name>`. It works (tested) but the npm package is stale (0.1.0, March 2025) and **drops files into `./components/motion-primitives/` at the project root, not `src/`**. Either move them to `src/components/motion-primitives/` or skip the CLI and copy the registry JSON content. Decide once and document it.\n102\t- Needs the `@` alias in both `vite.config.ts` (`resolve.alias`) and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths: { \"@/*\": [\"./src/*\"] }`). With TS 6/7, `paths` works **without** `baseUrl` (verified in the probe). shadcn's Vite doc still says to add `baseUrl`. You do not need it.\n103\t\n104\t### 2. react-spring : USE, scoped. Confidence: HIGH\n105\t\n106\t- Install `@react-spring/web@~10.1.2`. Import `useSpring, useSprings, useTrail, useChain, useTransition, animated, config` from `@react-spring/web`.\n107\t- Version note: `react-spring` (the umbrella, 10.0.4) is behind and v11.0.0-beta (beta.1 released 2026-10-01) is **removing it**, going ESM-only, and deprecating `useTransition` in favor of new `usePresence`/`usePresenceList`. Stay on 10.1.x stable. Avoid `useTransition` for new core UI (use motion's `AnimatePresence`) so the v11 migration stays trivial.\n108\t- Reduced motion is built in: `useReducedMotion` is exported from `@react-spring/web` (confirmed in the type definitions), and the global flag `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation: true })` snaps every spring to its target. Wire it once (snippet below).\n109\t- Dependencies are tiny and tree-shakable. Measured at about +17 kB gz for `useSpring/useTrail/useSprings`.\n110\t\n111\t### 3. realtimecolors.com : USE as a design tool, with caveats. Confidence: HIGH\n112\t\n113\t- The seed (`050315 / fbfbfe / 2f27ce / dedcff / 433bff`, Inter/Inter) is Text, Background, Primary, Secondary, Accent. Note `050315` on `fbfbfe` is the **light** theme pairing (dark text on light bg). The tool's light/dark toggle (Alt+T) lets you design both modes in one session. The project requires light and dark, so design both explicitly rather than inverting.\n114\t- It shows AA/AAA contrast traffic lights per role. Use them as the accessibility gate for text and interactive colors.\n115\t- Exports: CSS variables, Tailwind CSS, SCSS, and custom templates, in HEX/RGB/HSL/OKLAB/OKLCH. The tool lists a `tailwind.config.js` style Tailwind export and does not mention v4. **Export \"CSS\" with OKLCH and paste into `@theme`**, not the Tailwind-config export:\n116\t- Its type-scale presets (1.067 to 1.618) map to a `--text-*` ladder in `@theme`.\n117\t- The user wants alternatives explored: do that in the design phase (Realtime Colors' scheme generators: analogous/complementary/triadic, plus randomize with Space). Keep exactly 5 role tokens plus derived tints so swapping palettes later is a single-block edit.\n118\t\n119\t### 4. godly.design/posts : USE as inspiration only, not a dependency. Confidence: MEDIUM\n120\t\n121\t- `godly.design/posts` is a curated **feed of linked social posts** (mostly X links to designers' UI interaction clips), with category nav (Websites, Tools, Templates, etc.). No dates, tags, RSS, or API were visible. Treat it as a mood-board source: capture screenshots/links into the planning docs, then extract motion patterns (spring feel, press states, cursor effects) into a written reference list before coding. (Probe also noted `godly.website` currently 301-redirects to `recent.design`, so the brand's structure may have shifted. Rely on `godly.design/posts/` and verify when you actually browse.)\n122\t- No code or package implications.\n123\t\n124\t## Division of Responsibility (motion vs react-spring)\n125\t\n126\t| Concern | Owner | Why |\n127\t|---------|-------|-----|\n128\t| Mount/unmount, `AnimatePresence`, exit animations | **motion** | Mature, handles React 19 strict mode and transitions (13.1.1 and 13.4.6 fixes). |\n129\t| Layout and shared-element transitions (`layout`, `layoutId`, Morphing Dialog) | **motion** | react-spring has no equivalent. |\n130\t| Scroll-linked values (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, ScrollProgress, in-view reveals) | **motion** | Already bundled. 13.4.x moved offsets onto hardware-accelerated `ViewTimeline` where supported. |\n131\t| Text/number effects, stagger reveals, all motion-primitives prefabs | **motion** | They are written against it. |\n132\t| Simple drag, `whileTap`, `whileHover` | **motion** | Built in. No extra gesture lib. |\n133\t| Velocity-carrying physics: flick/toss, drag-release with momentum, rubber-band, pointer-chasing follower, squish-on-press toys | **react-spring** | `useSpring` with continuous retargeting is its sweet spot and gives the \"tactile\" signature. |\n134\t| Staggered chains of springs (`useTrail`, `useChain`) for playful sets of elements | **react-spring** | Concise API. |\n135\t| Anything in `@react-spring/three` | **react-spring** | Only if 3D is adopted. |\n136\t\n137\t## Tailwind v4 Notes\n138\t\n139\t- v4 `hover:` only applies under `@media (hover: hover)`, so hover styles never stick after a tap on phones. This matches the \"no desktop-only gestures\" requirement. Do **not** restore v3 behavior with `@custom-variant hover (&:hover)`.\n140\t- Browser floor: Safari 16.4+, Chrome 111+, Firefox 128+ (uses `@property`, `color-mix()`). Fine for this audience.\n141\t- No `tailwind.config.js`; `tw-animate-css` (the shadcn replacement for tailwindcss-animate) is not needed since motion handles animation.\n142\t\n143\t## Dark Mode (covered: Tailwind v4 + tiny script)\n144\t\n145\t## Font Loading\n146\t\n147\t- `import '@fontsource-variable/inter/wght.css'` in `main.tsx`. Verified: it declares `font-display: swap`, `font-weight: 100 900`, and per-script `unicode-range`, so a browser downloads only the **latin subset (48 kB woff2)** for English copy (latin-ext is 85 kB and only fetched if used). Vite emits all subsets to `dist/` (harmless unused files).\n148\t- `@fontsource-variable/inter/opsz.css` adds optical sizing (latin 73 kB). Worth it only if you want display-size tuning.\n149\t- **Preload is not automatic**: a bare `<link rel=\"preload\" href=\"@fontsource-variable/inter/files/...\">` in `index.html` is *not* rewritten by Vite (verified, stays unresolved). If you want a preload, use a small `transformIndexHtml` plugin or `import url from '...woff2?url'`. Skipping preload is acceptable with `font-display: swap`; revisit if Lighthouse flags it.\n150\t- If the design adds a display face from Realtime Colors, use the same Fontsource route (one variable family, latin only), and keep to 2 families total for mobile performance.\n151\t\n152\t## GitHub Pages Deploy\n153\t\n154\t## Alternatives Considered\n155\t\n156\t| Category | Recommended | Alternative | Why Not |\n157\t|----------|-------------|-------------|---------|\n158\t| Framework | Vite + React | Next.js / Astro | Next explicitly out of scope. Astro would be lighter on JS but the user wants React islands everywhere and these two React animation libs. |\n159\t| Animation split | motion for UI choreography + react-spring for signature physics | react-spring only | Loses `layoutId`/`AnimatePresence`/scroll and every motion-primitives component. |\n160\t| Animation split | (same) | motion only | Viable and smaller (saves 17 kB), but the user explicitly wants to try react-spring. Revisit if the spike shows react-spring adds nothing. |\n161\t| react-spring package | `@react-spring/web` | `react-spring` umbrella | Lagging, `deprecated` tag present, removed in v11 beta. |\n162\t| Installing primitives | Copy via registry JSON / CLI, then patch | Wait for an npm package | None exists. Source ownership is the model. |\n163\t| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind v3.4 | Only for pre-2023 browsers. motion-primitives and shadcn are on v4. |\n164\t| TypeScript | 6.0.x | 7.0.x | 7.0.2 compiled the probe cleanly, so it is available, but v6 is the scaffold default. Take 7 post-launch. |\n165\t| Dark mode | Tailwind data-attr + inline script | next-themes | Next-oriented abstraction we do not need. |\n166\t| Fonts | Fontsource self-hosted | Google Fonts `<link>` | Third-party request, render-blocking, privacy. |\n167\t\n168\t## What NOT to Use\n169\t\n170\t| Do not use | Why |\n171\t|------------|-----|\n172\t| `react-spring` umbrella package | Deprecated tag, removed in v11. Use `@react-spring/web`. |\n173\t| `react-spring` v11 beta | Beta, breaking `onRest`/`immediate` semantics. Wait for stable. |\n174\t| Raw `framer-motion` imports | motion 14 depends on `framer-motion` internally. Always import from `motion/react` so there is one copy. |\n175\t| motion-primitives `Cursor`, `Magnetic`, `Tilt`, `Spotlight` as *primary* interactions | Mouse-only. They are desktop garnish. |\n176\t| `shadcn init` | Unneeded; adds Radix/CVA you will not use. |\n177\t| `tailwind.config.js`, `@tailwind base/components/utilities` | v3 patterns. |\n178\t| `@use-gesture/react` by default | Last published March 2024, and motion already provides drag/tap/hover. Only add if a react-spring-driven drag cannot be done well otherwise. |\n179\t| `vite-plugin-image-optimizer` as the primary image tool | Compress-only (no srcset/format variants). Last publish Oct 2025. |\n180\t| Hard-coded absolute asset paths | Break when `base` changes. |\n181\t| Both libraries animating the same element/property | The one real coexistence hazard. |\n182\t\n183\t## Gaps: capabilities the four tools do NOT cover\n184\t\n185\t| Gap | Already covered? | Candidate A (default) | Candidate B | Notes |\n186\t|-----|------------------|-----------------------|-------------|-------|\n187\t| **Scroll-driven animation** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, `scroll()`, motion-primitives ScrollProgress/InView) | motion `useScroll` (+0 kB) | GSAP ScrollTrigger (+44 kB gz measured). GSAP is now fully free incl. plugins per gsap.com. | Only choose GSAP for pinned/scrubbed multi-step timelines. Not needed for six sections. |\n188\t| **Smooth scroll** | No | **None (native scroll)** | Lenis 1.3.26 (+5.6 kB measured). `lenis/react` provides `ReactLenis`. | Lenis smoothing is **off on touch by default** (`syncTouch: false`), honors reduced motion by default (`respectReducedMotion: true`), but needs `anchors: true` for in-page nav and community reports it can feel unlike native scroll and conflict with focus/keyboard behavior. Smooth scroll is a taste item, not a requirement. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EqAk77cJuTihZK8Ls8eim6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set and\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export); the og\n70\t|                            image and robots.txt are launch assets, not yet\n71\t|                            shipped\n72\t|-- index.html               meta description, inline theme-init script and the\n73\t|                            static hero shell; social tags and noscript\n74\t|                            content for the other sections arrive with the\n75\t|                            launch assets\n76\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n77\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n78\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n79\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n80\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n81\t`-- src/\n82\t    |-- main.tsx\n83\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n84\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n85\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n86\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n87\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n88\t    |-- components/\n89\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n90\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n91\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored and logged in an ADR (TextEffect, kept\n92\t    |                        unrendered for the hero role-line effect)\n93\t    |-- anim/\n94\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n95\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n96\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n97\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n98\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n99\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n100\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph)\n101\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n102\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n103\t    |-- content/\n104\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n105\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n106\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n107\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n108\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n109\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n110\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n111\t    |-- styles/\n112\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n113\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n114\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n115\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n116\t```\n117\t\n118\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n119\t\n120\t## Data flow\n121\t\n122\tBuild time:\n123\t\n124\t```text\n125\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n126\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n127\t```\n128\t\n129\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n130\t\n131\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n132\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n133\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n134\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n135\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n136\t\n137\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n138\t\n139\t## Content model\n140\t\n141\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n142\t\n143\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n144\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n145\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n146\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n147\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n148\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n149\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n150\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n151\t\n152\tRules:\n153\t\n154\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n155\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n156\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n157\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n158\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n159\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n160\t\n161\t## Design tokens\n162\t\n163\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n164\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n165\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n166\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n167\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n168\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n169\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n170\t\n171\t## Animation layer\n172\t\n173\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n174\t\n175\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n176\t|-------------------|-------|\n177\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n178\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n179\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n180\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n181\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n182\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n183\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n184\t\n185\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n186\t\n187\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n188\t\n189\t## Reduced motion\n190\t\n191\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n192\t\n193\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n194\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n195\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n196\t\n197\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs, and no wrapper applies a hidden initial state in CSS. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query, so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload in both directions. Turning reduced motion off mid-visit also loads the motion feature chunk, which reduced-motion visitors otherwise never download.\n198\t\n199\t## Assets\n200\t\n201\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n202\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n203\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n204\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n205\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n206\t\n207\t## Build and deploy\n208\t\n209\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n210\t\n211\t## Quality gates\n212\t\n213\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n214\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n215\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n216\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n217\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n218\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n219\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n220\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n221\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n222\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n223\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n224\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n225\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n226\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n227\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n228\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n229\t\n230\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n231\t\n232\t## Stack\n233\t\n234\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n235\t\n236\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n237\t|------------|---------|---------|\n238\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n239\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n240\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n241\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n242\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n243\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n244\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n245\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n246\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n247\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n248\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n249\t| pdfjs-dist | 6.3.289 (exact, dev only) | Phone-number gate: reads the served PDF's text, links and metadata |\n250\t| axe-core | 4.13.0 (dev only) | Accessibility pass of the visual QA run |\n251\t\n252\tUI glyphs are inline SVG (ADR-0010), so there is no icon package. Not used: the react-spring umbrella package, the react-spring v11 beta, raw framer-motion imports, shadcn init, tailwind.config.js, a client-side router, 3D or WebGL.\n253\t\n254\t## Decisions\n255\t\n256\tIndividual decisions, their status and the list of still-open decisions are tracked in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n257\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_018yoCgStsUeiiqg13uxyMAY","agent_id":"a7ba3c461334acbca","session_id":"703ce9a0-10ae-4d2a-b66a-717b3c791e25","uuid":"9cd94b23-a1b5-4ec6-9410-2f4d1078a4e3","timestamp":"2026-11-20T05:27:24.536Z","tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01EqAk77cJuTihZK8Ls8eim6","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}],"subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

No router, no global store, no data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- All copy and structured data is typed TypeScript in `src/content/`, validated by `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`. Components never hard-code site copy.\n- Single page. Sections are anchors on one page (`#hero`, `#experience`, `#projects`, `#about`, `#skills`, `#education`, `#contact`); the scroll-spy highlights the current one. Project details open in a native dialog overlay (ADR-0009).\n- Props flow down. Only three small React contexts exist: theme, motion preference, toast. Everything else is props from `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Import direction is enforced: dependencies point down only. Violations fail `npm run lint` through oxlint `no-restricted-imports` overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, as described in `docs/architecture.md`.\n- Theme is a single source of truth: the `data-theme` attribute on `<html>`. The inline script in `index.html` sets it before first paint; `src/lib/theme.ts` and `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` read and write it.\n- Motion is progressive. Content is visible without JavaScript; animation wrappers never apply hidden initial CSS states.\n\n## Layers\n\n**`app/` (composition root):**\n- Purpose: Mount React, wrap providers, assemble the page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Contains: Entry, provider tree, the page composition with the single scroll-spy call (`useActiveSection`).\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `components/ui/Toast`, `content/`, `hooks/`, `layout/`, `sections/`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` only\n\n**`sections/` (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: One folder per portfolio section; props in, JSX out.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Experience/Experience.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` (+ `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `src/sections/About/About.tsx`, `src/sections/Skills/Skills.tsx`, `src/sections/Education/Education.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Depends on: `layout/`, `components/ui/`, the `anim/` barrel, content types (types only, not data)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**`layout/` (chrome and structure):**\n- Purpose: Page frame, navigation, skip link, wordmark, footer, container and section wrappers.\n- Location: `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/Footer.tsx`, `src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`, `src/layout/Wordmark.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx`, `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `components/ui/`, `hooks/`, `lib/`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `sections/*`\n\n**`components/` (reusable UI and vendored code):**\n- Purpose: Shared UI primitives and vendored motion-primitives source.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (`CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/*.tsx` as inline SVG); `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (vendored, ADR-0007)\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `hooks/`, `lib/`; vendored code may import `motion/react` and `lib/utils`\n- Used by: `layout/`, `sections/`, `app/`\n\n**`anim/` (animation boundary):**\n- Purpose: The only layer that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`. Exposes semantic components and hooks.\n- Location: `src/anim/index.ts` (public barrel), `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts`, `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/` (`ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`), `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `components/motion-primitives/`, styles token mirror\n- Used by: everything above `anim/`, through `src/anim/index.ts` only\n\n**`hooks/` and `lib/` (shared logic):**\n- Purpose: Stateful hooks and pure helpers.\n- Location: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (IntersectionObserver scroll-spy, desktop/phone behavior), `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`; `src/lib/theme.ts` (theme read/write/sync, storage key), `src/lib/copy.ts` (copy-to-clipboard), `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn()`)\n- Depends on: browser APIs only\n- Used by: `app/`, `layout/`, `components/ui/`, `sections/`\n\n**`content/` (data):**\n- Purpose: Typed site content, one module per section, plus shared schema.\n- Location: `src/content/schema.ts` (all types), `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts` (image registry), `index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`)\n- Depends on: `schema.ts`, image imports\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only\n\n**`styles/` (design tokens and CSS):**\n- Purpose: Tailwind v4 entry, role tokens for light and dark, base resets, interaction states.\n- Location: `src/styles/index.css` (Tailwind import, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping), `src/styles/tokens.css` (five role colors per theme plus derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`), `src/styles/base.css` (resets, focus ring, `prefers-reduced-motion` guard), `src/styles/interactions.css`, `src/styles/not-found.css`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` imports `./styles/index.css`\n\n**Build and quality tooling:**\n- Purpose: Gates run by `npm run verify` and CI.\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`, `scripts/*.mjs`, `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`, `tests/unit/*`, `tests/e2e/*`, `playwright.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`. The inline script in `index.html` reads `localStorage['theme']`, falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, and sets `html[data-theme]` before paint.\n2. The static hero shell in `index.html` (`#root` contents) is visible before JavaScript runs.\n3. `index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx` (module script). `main.tsx` imports `@fontsource-variable/archivo/wdth.css` and `./styles/index.css`, then `createRoot(...).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)` (`src/main.tsx`).\n4. `App` (`src/app/App.tsx`) renders `Providers` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`) around `Page` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives the email from the `kind: 'email'` contact entry, filters the other contacts, and passes props to sections.\n6. `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`) attaches an IntersectionObserver and a `matchMedia` desktop query. Its `activeId` and `lockTo` feed `SiteHeader` (desktop nav) and `PillNav` (phone pill).\n\n### Theme Toggle Flow\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) calls `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`) writes `data-theme` first, then `localStorage['theme']`. If storage throws, the choice is kept in memory (`unsavedChoice`).\n3. `subscribeTheme` observes `data-theme` mutations, `storage` events from other tabs and OS scheme changes while nothing is stored.\n4. The physics thumb (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`, react-spring) animates the toggle.\n\n### Motion Preference Flow\n\n1. `MotionPreferenceProvider` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`) reads `useMotionPreference` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`), which watches `(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`.\n2. The provider wraps children in motion's `MotionConfig` and calls react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`.\n3. `MotionFeatures` (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`) loads the async motion feature chunk (`src/anim/features.ts`, status in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`) so `LazyMotion` can render. Reduced-motion visitors do not download it unless they turn reduced motion off mid-visit.\n\n### Project Detail Flow\n\n1. A click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` (or `Sleeve.tsx`) sets local state for the selected project.\n2. `ProjectDialog` (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`) renders the native `<dialog>` overlay with focus handling (ADR-0006, ADR-0009).\n\n### Copy Email Flow\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` (`src/lib/copy.ts`), which uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText` and falls back when unavailable. Toasts (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`) confirm the result.\n\n### Build-time Content Flow\n\n```text\nsrc/content/*.ts ──(typed, satisfies schema.ts)──> src/content/index.ts (siteContent)\n                                                          │\n                                                          ▼\n                                                  src/app/Page.tsx ──props──> sections ──props──> layout, ui\nsrc/assets/images (imagetools) ──hashed imports──> src/content/images.ts ──> image fields\n```\n\n**State Management:**\n- Three contexts: theme (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`), motion preference (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`), toast (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`).\n- Active section is local hook state in `Page`.\n- Project dialog selection is local state in the Projects section.\n- Persistent state is only `localStorage['theme']`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content types and modules:**\n- Purpose: Structured site data with a single schema.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `ContactLink`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`), `src/content/index.ts`\n- Pattern: `as const satisfies readonly ContactLink[]` in content modules; ordering by array order or a `featured` flag, never in components.\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Stable semantic API so sections never import motion libraries.\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts` exports `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`, `MotionFeatures`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`.\n- Pattern: One library per element/property. Motion for mount, layout, scroll and text effects; react-spring for velocity physics. Wrappers render an instant equivalent under reduced motion and forward `className`.\n\n**Design tokens:**\n- Purpose: One palette block drives every surface.\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css`; mirror values in `src/anim/tokens.ts` (springs, durations, eases).\n- Pattern: Five role tokens (text, background, primary, secondary, accent) authored separately per theme; derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`. No raw hex in components (`scripts/check-colors.mjs`).\n\n**Vendored primitive:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in motion-primitives component owned by this repo.\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (ADR-0007; not yet rendered in the hero)\n- Pattern: Treated as owned code. Excluded from lint autofix churn; may import `motion/react` and `lib/utils`.\n\n**Scroll-spy hook:**\n- Purpose: Single source of active section for both navs.\n- Examples: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (returns `activeId`, `lockTo`)\n- Pattern: IntersectionObserver with a click-lock so programmatic navigation does not flicker.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML entry (client app):**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: Browser request to `/` (or `${BASE_URL}`)\n- Responsibilities: Pre-JS hero shell, theme inline script, `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">`.\n\n**Not-found entry:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Rollup/Rolldown input in `vite.config.ts`, styles in `src/styles/not-found.css`, test `tests/e2e/not-found.spec.ts`)\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths.\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module script in `index.html`\n- Responsibilities: Font and style imports, `StrictMode` root render of `App`.\n\n**Build config entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `vite`, `vite build`, `vite preview`, Vitest and Playwright (via Vite config for `base`)\n- Responsibilities: `base` from `VITE_BASE`, plugins, `@` alias, two HTML inputs.\n\n**CI entries:**\n- Location: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`\n- Triggers: `push`, `pull_request`, `push` to `main`, `workflow_dispatch`\n- Responsibilities: `npm run verify`, `npm run test:e2e`, Pages build, deploy and smoke check.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers, no service worker, no server.\n- **Global state:** `src/lib/theme.ts` holds module-level `unsavedChoice` (let binding). Theme truth lives on `document.documentElement` `data-theme`. Motion globals are set via react-spring `Globals.assign` in the motion preference provider.\n- **Circular imports:** None documented in `docs/architecture.md`. Import direction is enforced by lint rules.\n- **Import boundaries:** Only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`. Only `src/app/Page.tsx` may import content data. Sections may not import content data or animation libraries directly.\n- **Build output:** Two HTML inputs (`index.html`, `404.html`); base path is environment-driven, so no absolute asset paths are used (`import.meta.env.BASE_URL` for `public/` files).\n- **Single-palette rule:** Components use semantic Tailwind utilities; raw color values live in `src/styles/tokens.css` and vendored primitives only.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries from sections or layout\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the single animation boundary and the reduced-motion and feature-chunk behavior in `src/anim/`. The oxlint `no-restricted-imports` rule fails CI.\n**Do this instead:** Import from the `@/anim` barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`) and add a semantic wrapper there if one is missing.\n\n### Importing content data outside `Page.tsx`\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout component imports from `@/content` (for example `siteContent`) and reads data itself.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the import rule in `docs/architecture.md`. Ordering and data decisions must stay in content and flow down as props.\n**Do this instead:** Add a prop on the section and pass the value from `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n### Writing theme state directly instead of through `lib/theme.ts`\n\n**What happens:** Code sets `data-theme` or `localStorage['theme']` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Bypasses blocked-storage handling and the cross-tab/OS sync in `subscribeTheme`.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` (or the `useTheme` hook).\n\n### Animating one element with both libraries\n\n**What happens:** The same element or property is animated by both motion and react-spring, or by a Tailwind `transition-*` utility plus a library.\n**Why it's wrong:** The two systems fight over the same property (`docs/architecture.md`, Animation layer).\n**Do this instead:** Pick one owner per interaction using the class table in `docs/architecture.md`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive, degrade-to-visible. Storage, `matchMedia` and clipboard access are wrapped in `try`/`catch` or feature checks, and fall back to a working default.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `localStorage` access in `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html` is wrapped in try/catch; blocked storage keeps the choice for the session.\n- `window.matchMedia` access is guarded in `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` and `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`; a missing API means no OS-follow or a default value.\n- Clipboard copy in `src/lib/copy.ts` checks `navigator.clipboard?.writeText` before calling it.\n- No global error boundary or error reporting service is configured.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the app. Build and QA scripts log to the console.\n**Validation:** Compile-time via `satisfies` and `tsc -b`; content tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`; build gates in `scripts/`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable. Static site.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), `aria-labelledby` on sections, focus ring in `src/styles/base.css`, WCAG 2.2 AA contrast test (`scripts/check-contrast.mjs`, `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`), reduced-motion handling across all three animation systems.\n**Motion preference:** One source (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) feeding motion `MotionConfig`, react-spring `Globals`, and a CSS guard in `src/styles/base.css`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2967,"cache_read_input_tokens":73572,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2967,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_018yoCgStsUeiiqg13uxyMAY","agent_id":"a7ba3c461334acbca","session_id":"703ce9a0-10ae-4d2a-b66a-717b3c791e25","uuid":"be44de3e-a920-408a-8270-ba348e7c8de8","timestamp":"2026-11-20T05:28:36.422Z","request_id":"req_011ChDdM3aPzySpBKVS4JBCX","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

mobile/ subfolder\n│   └── fixtures/                     # lint-boundary fixtures (anim, app, sections, motion-primitives)\n├── index.html                        # HTML entry 1 (pre-JS hero shell, theme script)\n├── 404.html                          # HTML entry 2 (GitHub Pages not-found)\n├── package.json / package-lock.json  # npm manifest and lockfile\n├── vite.config.ts                    # Vite config (base, plugins, alias, two inputs)\n├── vitest.config.ts                  # Vitest config\n├── playwright.config.ts              # Playwright config\n├── prettier.config.mjs               # Prettier config\n├── .oxlintrc.json / .oxlintignore    # oxlint rules (import boundaries) and ignores\n├── tsconfig.json                     # TS project root\n├── tsconfig.app.json                 # app sources\n├── tsconfig.node.json                # Vite/Node config files\n├── tsconfig.scripts.json             # scripts/\n├── tsconfig.test.json                # tests/\n└── .gitignore\n```\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: Application root and page composition\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Key files: `src/app/Page.tsx` (only file that imports content data; wires `useActiveSection`, nav and sections)\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: The only code that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`\n- Contains: providers, preference hook, feature loader, tokens, declarative and physics wrappers\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts` (public barrel), `src/anim/tokens.ts` (spring and duration tokens), `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`\n- Subfolders: `declarative/` (motion-owned: `ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`); `physics/` (react-spring-owned: `ThemeThumb.tsx`)\n\n**`src/components/`:**\n- Purpose: Shared components\n- `ui/`: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`\n- `ui/icons/`: inline SVG components - `GitHubIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`, `ChevronLeftIcon.tsx`, `ChevronRightIcon.tsx`, `CloseIcon.tsx`, `DownloadIcon.tsx`\n- `motion-primitives/`: vendored source, `text-effect.tsx` (see ADR-0007). Do not add new primitives without an ADR.\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: All site copy and structured data\n- Contains: `schema.ts` (all types), one module per section (`profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`), `images.ts` (image registry), `index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`)\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: Stateful React hooks shared across layers\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: Page frame and navigation\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx` (desktop header and nav), `PillNav.tsx` (phone pill nav), `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS` and nav definitions)\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Framework-free helpers\n- Contains: `theme.ts` (storage key, read/apply/subscribe), `copy.ts` (clipboard), `utils.ts` (`cn()`)\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: One folder per portfolio section, each a presentational component fed by props\n- Folders: `Hero/`, `Experience/`, `Projects/`, `About/`, `Skills/`, `Education/`, `Contact/`\n- Projects folder also contains `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `project-types.ts`\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Tailwind v4 entry and design tokens\n- Contains: `index.css` (imports Tailwind, `@custom-variant`, `@theme inline`), `tokens.css` (role and derived color tokens for light and dark), `base.css` (resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard), `interactions.css` (hover, press and CTA states), `not-found.css` (404 page)\n\n**`src/types/`:**\n- Purpose: Ambient type declarations\n- Contains: `imagetools.d.ts`\n\n**`public/`:**\n- Purpose: Files that must keep a stable URL. Referenced from code via `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n- Contains: `favicon.svg`, `resume.pdf`\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: Node ESM quality gates run by `npm run verify`\n- Contains: `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs` (with `contrast-pairs.json`), `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs`\n\n**`tests/`:**\n- Purpose: Test suites, separate from `src/`\n- `tests/unit/`: `active-section`, `boundaries`, `content`, `content-inventory`, `copy`, `gates`, `hero-parity`, `layout`, `motion-features`, `motion-preference`, `theme`, `theme-script`, `tokens`, `use-theme` (`.test.ts` and `.test.tsx`)\n- `tests/e2e/`: `hero`, `nav`, `theme`, `motion`, `contact`, `content`, `copy-email`, `project-dialog`, `resume`, `smoke`, `wordmark`, `not-found` specs; `mobile/` (`pill`, `ten-second`); `global-setup.ts`\n- `tests/setup.ts`: Vitest setup\n- `tests/fixtures/`: sample files used by lint-boundary tests\n\n**`docs/`:**\n- Purpose: Public engineering documentation\n- Contains: `architecture.md`, `deploy-runbook.md`, `contrast-matrix.md`, `adr/` (ADR-0001 through ADR-0010 plus `adr/README.md`)\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: GSD planning artifacts (PROJECT, REQUIREMENTS, ROADMAP, STATE, WINDOWS, `phases/NN-name/`, `codebase/`)\n- Generated: No (hand-written and GSD-generated)\n- Committed: Yes (ADR-0008 governs visibility)\n\n**`.github/workflows/`:**\n- Purpose: CI and Pages deploy\n- Key files: `ci.yml`, `deploy.yml`\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: HTML shell and theme script\n- `404.html`: not-found HTML entry\n- `src/main.tsx`: React root\n- `src/app/App.tsx`: app root component\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: page composition\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: build and dev config\n- `package.json`: scripts and dependencies\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: app TypeScript config\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint rules and import boundaries\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: formatting\n- `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`: test runners\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: CI and deploy\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/anim/index.ts`: animation API surface\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme persistence and sync\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy\n- `src/content/schema.ts`: content types\n- `src/styles/tokens.css`: color tokens\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: unit and gate tests\n- `tests/e2e/`: browser tests\n- `tests/setup.ts`: Vitest setup\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx` - `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`\n- Hooks: camelCase with `use` prefix - `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`\n- Non-component modules: camelCase `.ts` - `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/content/profile.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Mixed: kebab-case for some non-component modules - `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`\n- Icons: `<Name>Icon.tsx` in `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Tests: `<name>.test.ts(x)` in `tests/unit/` (kebab-case); `<topic>.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Scripts: `check-<thing>.mjs` in `scripts/`\n- ADRs: `NNNN-kebab-title.md` in `docs/adr/`\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section and feature folders: PascalCase under `src/sections/` (`Hero`, `Projects`)\n- Infrastructure folders: lowercase single word under `src/` (`app`, `anim`, `lib`, `hooks`, `layout`, `content`, `styles`, `types`)\n- Test folders: lowercase under `tests/`\n\n**Code naming:**\n- Components: named exports (`export function Page()`, `export function App()`)\n- Module constants: UPPER_SNAKE (`THEME_STORAGE_KEY`, `DARK_QUERY`, `SECTION_IDS`)\n- Content modules: `as const satisfies <Type>[]`\n- Path alias: `@/` maps to `src/` (`vite.config.ts`, `tsconfig.app.json`); use `@/anim`, `@/content`, `@/layout/...`\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx` (props in, JSX out)\n- Data: new field on `src/content/schema.ts`, data in `src/content/<name>.ts`, wired through `src/content/index.ts`\n- Render: add to `src/app/Page.tsx` (the only place that reads content)\n- Nav entry: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Tests: `tests/unit/<name>.test.tsx` and optionally `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`\n\n**New animated interaction:**\n- Wrapper: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx` (motion) or `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx` (react-spring)\n- Export from `src/anim/index.ts` (the only import path for sections and layout)\n- Tokens: add to `src/anim/tokens.ts`; mirror CSS variables in `src/styles/tokens.css` if CSS needs them\n- Must read `useMotionPreference` and render an instant equivalent when reduced\n\n**New UI component (shared):**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`\n- Icon: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx` as inline SVG (ADR-0010). No icon package.\n\n**New layout chrome:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`\n\n**New hook:**\n- `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts` for cross-section hooks; `src/components/ui/use<Name>.ts` if UI-only\n\n**New utility or helper:**\n- Pure helpers: `src/lib/<name>.ts`\n- Class names: use `cn()` from `src/lib/utils.ts`\n\n**New content data:**\n- Type first in `src/content/schema.ts`, then a module in `src/content/`, then add to `src/content/index.ts`\n- Image assets: `src/assets/images/` (imported as modules so Vite hashes them) and registered in `src/content/images.ts`. Confirm this directory exists before adding files.\n\n**New test:**\n- Unit: `tests/unit/<kebab-name>.test.ts(x)`\n- E2E: `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`; mobile-specific under `tests/e2e/mobile/`\n- Lint-boundary fixtures: `tests/fixtures/`\n\n**New static file with stable URL:**\n- `public/<file>`, referenced via `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`\n\n**New style token:**\n- Role color or derived token: `src/styles/tokens.css` (both light and dark blocks)\n- Tailwind mapping: `src/styles/index.css` (`@theme inline`)\n- No raw hex in components\n\n**New ADR:**\n- `docs/adr/NNNN-kebab-title.md` with a `Status:` line, and an entry in `docs/adr/README.md`\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`node_modules/`:**\n- Purpose: npm dependencies\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No (ignored by `.gitignore`)\n\n**`dist/`:**\n- Purpose: Vite build output uploaded by the Pages workflow (`./dist`)\n- Generated: Yes (`npm run build`)\n- Committed: No\n\n**`test-results/` and Playwright reports:**\n- Purpose: Playwright artifacts\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No\n\n**`.planning/codebase/`:**\n- Purpose: Codebase map documents (this folder)\n- Generated: Yes (by `/gsd-map-codebase`)\n- Committed: Per project policy\n\n**`.planning/phases/`:**\n- Purpose: Per-phase PLAN, SUMMARY, REVIEW, VERIFICATION and UAT files\n- Generated: Mixed (plans written, summaries and reviews generated)\n- Committed: Yes\n\n---\n\n*Structure analysis:
