Rollup/Rolldown input","newString":"second Rolldown input","originalFile":"<!-- refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Build time (Vite 8, npm run build)               │\n│  content/*.ts ──satisfies──> schema.ts    assets ──imagetools──> │\n│  index.html + 404.html (two HTML entries, vite.config.ts)        │\n└───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘\n                                ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Browser (static SPA, no router)                  │\n│                                                                  │\n│  index.html inline script ──> html[data-theme] before paint      │\n│                                                                  │\n│  src/main.tsx ──> src/app/App.tsx                                │\n│     Providers: MotionPreferenceProvider > MotionFeatures >       │\n│                ToastProvider                                     │\n│     Page.tsx (only file that reads content data)                 │\n│       ├─ SiteHeader / PillNav  (layout/, scroll-spy + nav)        │\n│       ├─ sections: Hero, Experience, Projects, About,            │\n│       │            Skills, Education, Contact                    │\n│       └─ Footer                                                  │\n│                                                                  │\n│  anim/ (only place importing motion/react, @react-spring/web)    │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, zero-backend, single-page React app with typed content modules, CSS-variable design tokens, and one internal animation layer. No router, no global store, no data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- All copy and structured data is typed TypeScript in `src/content/`, validated by `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`. Components never hard-code site copy.\n- Single page. Sections are anchors on one page (`#hero`, `#experience`, `#projects`, `#about`, `#skills`, `#education`, `#contact`); the scroll-spy highlights the current one. Project details open in a native dialog overlay (ADR-0009).\n- Props flow down. Only three small React contexts exist: theme, motion preference, toast. Everything else is props from `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Import direction is enforced: dependencies point down only. Violations fail `npm run lint` through oxlint `no-restricted-imports` overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, as described in `docs/architecture.md`.\n- Theme is a single source of truth: the `data-theme` attribute on `<html>`. The inline script in `index.html` sets it before first paint; `src/lib/theme.ts` and `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` read and write it.\n- Motion is progressive. Content is visible without JavaScript; animation wrappers never apply hidden initial CSS states.\n\n## Layers\n\n**`app/` (composition root):**\n- Purpose: Mount React, wrap providers, assemble the page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Contains: Entry, provider tree, the page composition with the single scroll-spy call (`useActiveSection`).\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `components/ui/Toast`, `content/`, `hooks/`, `layout/`, `sections/`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` only\n\n**`sections/` (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: One folder per portfolio section; props in, JSX out.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Experience/Experience.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` (+ `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `src/sections/About/About.tsx`, `src/sections/Skills/Skills.tsx`, `src/sections/Education/Education.tsx`, `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`\n- Depends on: `layout/`, `components/ui/`, the `anim/` barrel, content types (types only, not data)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**`layout/` (chrome and structure):**\n- Purpose: Page frame, navigation, skip link, wordmark, footer, container and section wrappers.\n- Location: `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/Footer.tsx`, `src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`, `src/layout/Wordmark.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx`, `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`)\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `components/ui/`, `hooks/`, `lib/`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `sections/*`\n\n**`components/` (reusable UI and vendored code):**\n- Purpose: Shared UI primitives and vendored motion-primitives source.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (`CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/*.tsx` as inline SVG); `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (vendored, ADR-0007)\n- Depends on: `anim/` barrel, `hooks/`, `lib/`; vendored code may import `motion/react` and `lib/utils`\n- Used by: `layout/`, `sections/`, `app/`\n\n**`anim/` (animation boundary):**\n- Purpose: The only layer that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`. Exposes semantic components and hooks.\n- Location: `src/anim/index.ts` (public barrel), `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts`, `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/` (`ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`), `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `components/motion-primitives/`, styles token mirror\n- Used by: everything above `anim/`, through `src/anim/index.ts` only\n\n**`hooks/` and `lib/` (shared logic):**\n- Purpose: Stateful hooks and pure helpers.\n- Location: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (IntersectionObserver scroll-spy, desktop/phone behavior), `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`; `src/lib/theme.ts` (theme read/write/sync, storage key), `src/lib/copy.ts` (copy-to-clipboard), `src/lib/utils.ts` (`cn()`)\n- Depends on: browser APIs only\n- Used by: `app/`, `layout/`, `components/ui/`, `sections/`\n\n**`content/` (data):**\n- Purpose: Typed site content, one module per section, plus shared schema.\n- Location: `src/content/schema.ts` (all types), `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts` (image registry), `index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`)\n- Depends on: `schema.ts`, image imports\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only\n\n**`styles/` (design tokens and CSS):**\n- Purpose: Tailwind v4 entry, role tokens for light and dark, base resets, interaction states.\n- Location: `src/styles/index.css` (Tailwind import, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping), `src/styles/tokens.css` (five role colors per theme plus derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`), `src/styles/base.css` (resets, focus ring, `prefers-reduced-motion` guard), `src/styles/interactions.css`, `src/styles/not-found.css`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` imports `./styles/index.css`\n\n**Build and quality tooling:**\n- Purpose: Gates run by `npm run verify` and CI.\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`, `scripts/*.mjs`, `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`, `tests/unit/*`, `tests/e2e/*`, `playwright.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`. The inline script in `index.html` reads `localStorage['theme']`, falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, and sets `html[data-theme]` before paint.\n2. The static hero shell in `index.html` (`#root` contents) is visible before JavaScript runs.\n3. `index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx` (module script). `main.tsx` imports `@fontsource-variable/archivo/wdth.css` and `./styles/index.css`, then `createRoot(...).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)` (`src/main.tsx`).\n4. `App` (`src/app/App.tsx`) renders `Providers` (`src/app/Providers.tsx`) around `Page` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives the email from the `kind: 'email'` contact entry, filters the other contacts, and passes props to sections.\n6. `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` (`src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`) attaches an IntersectionObserver and a `matchMedia` desktop query. Its `activeId` and `lockTo` feed `SiteHeader` (desktop nav) and `PillNav` (phone pill).\n\n### Theme Toggle Flow\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) calls `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` (`src/lib/theme.ts`) writes `data-theme` first, then `localStorage['theme']`. If storage throws, the choice is kept in memory (`unsavedChoice`).\n3. `subscribeTheme` observes `data-theme` mutations, `storage` events from other tabs and OS scheme changes while nothing is stored.\n4. The physics thumb (`src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`, react-spring) animates the toggle.\n\n### Motion Preference Flow\n\n1. `MotionPreferenceProvider` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`) reads `useMotionPreference` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`), which watches `(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`.\n2. The provider wraps children in motion's `MotionConfig` and calls react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`.\n3. `MotionFeatures` (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`) loads the async motion feature chunk (`src/anim/features.ts`, status in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`) so `LazyMotion` can render. Reduced-motion visitors do not download it unless they turn reduced motion off mid-visit.\n\n### Project Detail Flow\n\n1. A click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` (or `Sleeve.tsx`) sets local state for the selected project.\n2. `ProjectDialog` (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`) renders the native `<dialog>` overlay with focus handling (ADR-0006, ADR-0009).\n\n### Copy Email Flow\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` (`src/lib/copy.ts`), which uses `navigator.clipboard.writeText` and falls back when unavailable. Toasts (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`) confirm the result.\n\n### Build-time Content Flow\n\n```text\nsrc/content/*.ts ──(typed, satisfies schema.ts)──> src/content/index.ts (siteContent)\n                                                          │\n                                                          ▼\n                                                  src/app/Page.tsx ──props──> sections ──props──> layout, ui\nsrc/assets/images (imagetools) ──hashed imports──> src/content/images.ts ──> image fields\n```\n\n**State Management:**\n- Three contexts: theme (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`), motion preference (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`), toast (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`).\n- Active section is local hook state in `Page`.\n- Project dialog selection is local state in the Projects section.\n- Persistent state is only `localStorage['theme']`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content types and modules:**\n- Purpose: Structured site data with a single schema.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `ContactLink`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`), `src/content/index.ts`\n- Pattern: `as const satisfies readonly ContactLink[]` in content modules; ordering by array order or a `featured` flag, never in components.\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Stable semantic API so sections never import motion libraries.\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts` exports `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`, `MotionFeatures`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`.\n- Pattern: One library per element/property. Motion for mount, layout, scroll and text effects; react-spring for velocity physics. Wrappers render an instant equivalent under reduced motion and forward `className`.\n\n**Design tokens:**\n- Purpose: One palette block drives every surface.\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css`; mirror values in `src/anim/tokens.ts` (springs, durations, eases).\n- Pattern: Five role tokens (text, background, primary, secondary, accent) authored separately per theme; derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`. No raw hex in components (`scripts/check-colors.mjs`).\n\n**Vendored primitive:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in motion-primitives component owned by this repo.\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (ADR-0007; not yet rendered in the hero)\n- Pattern: Treated as owned code. Excluded from lint autofix churn; may import `motion/react` and `lib/utils`.\n\n**Scroll-spy hook:**\n- Purpose: Single source of active section for both navs.\n- Examples: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` (returns `activeId`, `lockTo`)\n- Pattern: IntersectionObserver with a click-lock so programmatic navigation does not flicker.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML entry (client app):**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: Browser request to `/` (or `${BASE_URL}`)\n- Responsibilities: Pre-JS hero shell, theme inline script, `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">`.\n\n**Not-found entry:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Rollup/Rolldown input in `vite.config.ts`, styles in `src/styles/not-found.css`, test `tests/e2e/not-found.spec.ts`)\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths.\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Module script in `index.html`\n- Responsibilities: Font and style imports, `StrictMode` root render of `App`.\n\n**Build config entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `vite`, `vite build`, `vite preview`, Vitest and Playwright (via Vite config for `base`)\n- Responsibilities: `base` from `VITE_BASE`, plugins, `@` alias, two HTML inputs.\n\n**CI entries:**\n- Location: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`\n- Triggers: `push`, `pull_request`, `push` to `main`, `workflow_dispatch`\n- Responsibilities: `npm run verify`, `npm run test:e2e`, Pages build, deploy and smoke check.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers, no service worker, no server.\n- **Global state:** `src/lib/theme.ts` holds module-level `unsavedChoice` (let binding). Theme truth lives on `document.documentElement` `data-theme`. Motion globals are set via react-spring `Globals.assign` in the motion preference provider.\n- **Circular imports:** None documented in `docs/architecture.md`. Import direction is enforced by lint rules.\n- **Import boundaries:** Only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`. Only `src/app/Page.tsx` may import content data. Sections may not import content data or animation libraries directly.\n- **Build output:** Two HTML inputs (`index.html`, `404.html`); base path is environment-driven, so no absolute asset paths are used (`import.meta.env.BASE_URL` for `public/` files).\n- **Single-palette rule:** Components use semantic Tailwind utilities; raw color values live in `src/styles/tokens.css` and vendored primitives only.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries from sections or layout\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the single animation boundary and the reduced-motion and feature-chunk behavior in `src/anim/`. The oxlint `no-restricted-imports` rule fails CI.\n**Do this instead:** Import from the `@/anim` barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`) and add a semantic wrapper there if one is missing.\n\n### Importing content data outside `Page.tsx`\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout component imports from `@/content` (for example `siteContent`) and reads data itself.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the import rule in `docs/architecture.md`. Ordering and data decisions must stay in content and flow down as props.\n**Do this instead:** Add a prop on the section and pass the value from `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n### Writing theme state directly instead of through `lib/theme.ts`\n\n**What happens:** Code sets `data-theme` or `localStorage['theme']` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Bypasses blocked-storage handling and the cross-tab/OS sync in `subscribeTheme`.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` (or the `useTheme` hook).\n\n### Animating one element with both libraries\n\n**What happens:** The same element or property is animated by both motion and react-spring, or by a Tailwind `transition-*` utility plus a library.\n**Why it's wrong:** The two systems fight over the same property (`docs/architecture.md`, Animation layer).\n**Do this instead:** Pick one owner per interaction using the class table in `docs/architecture.md`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive, degrade-to-visible. Storage, `matchMedia` and clipboard access are wrapped in `try`/`catch` or feature checks, and fall back to a working default.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `localStorage` access in `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html` is wrapped in try/catch; blocked storage keeps the choice for the session.\n- `window.matchMedia` access is guarded in `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` and `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`; a missing API means no OS-follow or a default value.\n- Clipboard copy in `src/lib/copy.ts` checks `navigator.clipboard?.writeText` before calling it.\n- No global error boundary or error reporting service is configured.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the app. Build and QA scripts log to the console.\n**Validation:** Compile-time via `satisfies` and `tsc -b`; content tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`; build gates in `scripts/`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable. Static site.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), `aria-labelledby` on sections, focus ring in `src/styles/base.css`, WCAG 2.2 AA contrast test (`scripts/check-contrast.mjs`, `scripts/contrast-pairs.json`), reduced-motion handling across all three animation systems.\n**Motion preference:** One source (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) feeding motion `MotionConfig`, react-spring `Globals`, and a CSS guard in `src/styles/base.css`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":180,"oldLines":7,"newStart":180,"newLines":7,"lines":[" - Responsibilities: Pre-JS hero shell, theme inline script, `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">`."," "," **Not-found entry:**","-- Location: `404.html` (second Rollup/Rolldown input in `vite.config.ts`, styles in `src/styles/not-found.css`, test `tests/e2e/not-found.spec.ts`)","+- Location: `404.html` (second Rolldown input in `vite.config.ts`, styles in `src/styles/not-found.css`, test `tests/e2e/not-found.spec.ts`)"," - Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths."," "," **JS entry:**"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01VfABfxTnU47tWK2NrEvZW7","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}]}
